EARLIER AFTERNOON-ONLY VERSION: PING1 research (shared backtest code: HistData M1 bid, Apr-2025..Sep-2026; costs: per-pair spread x session factor, $0.06 commission, 0.2 p entry slip, 0.3 p stop slip, swap; Owner's net trailing ladder simulated with a 1-bar delay and the breathing-room rule; SL checked before the trail moves inside each M1 bar; max 6 open, 1 per pair). OUT-OF-SAMPLE Apr..Sep 2026: 418 trades, 3.2/day, 66.0% green, net -26.88, PF 0.813, avg win $0.423 vs avg loss $1.012, max DD $39.29, worst $-5.3. In-sample Apr-2025..Mar-2026: 3.4/day, 70.3% green, net -27.71, PF 0.918, max DD $62.79. Spread x2 stress: IS -99.03 / OOS -38.45. HONEST: NEGATIVE. Every M1-M4 variant tested was net negative after costs (trend-direction V: PF 0.55-0.70, about -$200 to -$1,100 per 6 months); this is the least-bad tweak (V turn after an M4 stretch, afternoon only). With zero costs the plain V is about break-even, so the spread + $0.06 commission is what sinks it. Expected live: auto-pause after ~30 closes if still negative. Caveat: Yahoo M1/M3 bars are noisier than the broker feed for EURUSD/GBPUSD/AUDUSD/NZDUSD/USDCAD (RSI correlation ~0.6-0.8).
0.01 lot · tag PING1 · PING1 v1.1 (Owner's V on tiny timeframes): direction = snap-back after a stretched M4 bar (RSI(2) of M4 < 5 -> up, > 95 -> down, valid 3 M4 bars); entry at the M3 'V' turn - the previous M3 bar made the 5-bar low, this M3 bar is green, closes above the previous bar's high with a higher low -> BUY (inverted V -> SELL). 9 lowest-spread pairs, entries ALL HOURS FX is open (no entries 4:45-6:15 PM ET rollover or Friday after 3:30 PM); 0.01 lot, hard SL 20x ATR14(M3) (max $3), no fixed TP: Owner's net trailing ladder moves the broker SL up (+$0.03 -> lock $0.01 ... +$1 -> $0.80, then 80% of peak) with breathing room 3x ATR14(M1); time stop 8 h; max 6 open, 1 per pair; tag PING1 · runner running · placing ON · last check Fri 10/9 9:15 AM ET
Entry: a ‘V’ on tiny timeframes. First an M4 (4-minute) bar is stretched: RSI(2) below 5 means look for a bounce up, above 95 means look for a drop (valid for 3 M4 bars). Then on M3 (3-minute) bars: the previous bar made the lowest low of the last 5 bars, and the new bar is green, closes above the previous bar’s high with a higher low → BUY (upside-down V → SELL). Pairs: 9 lowest-spread pairs (EURUSD, GBPUSD, USDJPY, USDCHF, USDCAD, AUDUSD, NZDUSD, EURGBP, EURCHF). Hours: all hours FX is open, except no entries 4:45–6:15 PM ET (rollover) and Friday after 3:30 PM. Size: 0.01 lot, max 6 open, 1 per pair. Ladder: once a trade is up +$0.03 net it locks +$0.01, rising to lock $0.80 at +$1, then 80% of the peak; the broker SL is moved up (or a virtual lock closes at market). Stop: hard broker stop 20× ATR(M3), at most $3 loss; time stop 8 h. Auto-stop: pauses at −$150 closed, 6 losses in a row, or still negative after 30 closes (now: $156.18 room, 6 losses-in-a-row left).

AI Consistent — behaviour library

AI Consistent — AI brain

brain heartbeat Fri 9:15:00 AM ET · local models, CPU only, no API keys
Its own intelligence, running on this box without a human: a forecaster, a news reader and a manager LLM. They feed the bandit and grow the behaviour pool. Nothing they suggest goes live untested: filters switch on only if the honest backtest shows edge after costs, and new behaviours must beat the incumbent on hidden data. The brain never places orders.

Manager proposals and decisions

Manager round Fri 7:15:23 AM ET · 380.3 s thinking

Analysis: The current strategy suffers from poor median trade profitability and MTM consistency due to low win/loss ratios and high costs. Existing rules are overly aggressive in timeframes (M30 PM) and lack optimization for low-spread, high-probability pairs like USDCAD and EURCHF. A shift toward tighter, trend-aligned RSI(2) fades with no-progress exits and capped stops can reduce noise and preserve capital. The best edge is found in pairs with strong sentiment and low volatility, such as USDCAD during NY session, where trend alignment and low spread combine to improve net returns after costs.
Lesson added to research notes: Trend-aligned RSI(2) fades on M30 PM in low-spread pairs during high-liquidity hours outperform random entries by reducing false breakouts and improving win/loss balance.

0.01 lot · tag XCLEAN · Xclean (strict control for Co-op 1, 0.01 lot FIXED). ONE frozen setup: M5 V-turn equilibrium after an M15 RSI(2) dip, 9 low-spread pairs. Entry = V-turn equilibrium: DIP (RSI(2) of the direction TF < 5 or > 95 in its last 3 bars and a 5-bar low/high), RISE (next bar closes through the dip bar by >= 0.2 x ATR, in its outer third, higher low/lower high), SETTLE (next bar holds above the turn bar's low/below its high, closes on the right side of its midpoint, range <= turn bar range), then ENTER at market. Volatility filter: skip when ATR(14) is in the top 20% of its last 100 bars. Boat (balance governor): max 4 open, 1 per pair, no more than 2 open trades leaning the same way on any one currency; while the test's total-if-closed is below $0 no new entries except one recovery trade when flat. Exits: hard stop 3 x ATR14 of the entry TF, never more than $2 incl. commission; Jim's trailing ladder with breathing room (+$0.03 -> lock $0.01, $0.15 -> $0.10, $0.30 -> $0.15, $0.50 -> $0.25, $1.00 -> $0.80, then 80% of peak); no-progress exit if +$0.03 not reached in 30 min; max hold 4 h; Friday 4:00 PM ET flatten. Auto-stop: -$150 closed or 5 losses in a row -> flatten and pause (awaiting orders). FTMO guards: equity < Prague day-start - $8,000 or < $182,000 -> flatten, no entries. Hours: all FX hours except 4:45-6:15 PM ET rollover; Friday no entries from 3:30 PM ET. NO learning library, NO mid-run rule changes: CONFIG + runner code + checklist are SHA-256 hashed at start and shown on the dashboard; any change is visible. Backtest (honest; simulated, not live trades): HistData M1 BID bars Apr-Sep 2026 (6 months, 13 crosses; only bid M1 data exists on the box - no historical ask or ticks; live median spreads from the 10/8 reality check used as cost). Filled in the SAME minute as the gap: 655 trades, net +50.22 USD, 52.7% green, PF 1.36 (A 40 trades +13.01; Z 615 trades +37.21). Filled ONE minute later: 634 trades, net -65.05 USD, 45.3% green, PF 0.61. So the gaps exist (about 5 a day across the 13 crosses) but the edge is gone if the fill is ~1 minute late; live fills take ~5-10 s through the terminal, so expect somewhere in between and likely around break-even or worse. M1 closes are not simultaneous across pairs, which makes the same-minute result optimistic. Armed anyway on Owner order 1:19 PM ET 10/8.
Reality check (read-only scan, no orders) (1:23–1:43 PM ET 10/8, 20 min, 379 reads of 13 crosses): 379 live reads over 20 min (median read 802 ms). A cross's gap beat its own cost (spread + commission) on 23.0% of cross-reads, but almost all of that is a STANDING broker offset, not a passing mistake: EURCAD -3.1 p on 100% of reads, CADJPY +2.2 p on 100% of reads, AUDCAD -1.9 p on 100% of reads (USDCAD.sim is priced ~2.5 p away from what EURCAD, AUDCAD and CADJPY imply, all the time). Fresh gaps beyond cost + 0.5 p: 0.0% of cross-reads. Under the armed rules the window would have fired trigger A 0 time(s) and trigger Z 0 time(s) (counted per read, so one episode can count several times). Every other cross: median gap 0.11 p vs cost 1.66 p. |z| reached 3+ on 12 of 13 crosses (biggest: AUDJPY z -4.3, EURAUD -4.0, EURCHF -3.8), but those moves were only 0.3-1.0 pip while cost was 1.1-2.8 pips, so none was big enough to pay for the trade. Expect very few trades.

0.01 lot · tag MISTAKE1 · Pricing-mistake hunter (FX version of two-pool arbitrage; no direction guess). Every ~3 s it reads the broker's own bid/ask for the 7 USD majors and 13 crosses from the MT5 terminal, works out what each cross SHOULD cost from its two USD legs (e.g. EURJPY = EURUSD x USDJPY), and measures the gap in pips. Trigger A (absolute mistake): the gap is bigger than the cross's spread + the $0.06 commission (in pips) + 0.5 pip safety, on 2 reads in a row, and it is NEW (a gap that has sat there for the last ~10 min is a standing broker offset, not a mistake - e.g. USDCAD.sim runs ~2.5 pips above what the CAD crosses imply). Trigger Z (statistical): the gap is 3+ standard deviations from its own ~10-minute average AND that move beats spread + commission. It then trades ONLY the cross, 0.01 lot (broker minimum), in the direction that closes the gap. Exit: as soon as the gap closes (back to zero for A, back to its average for Z) and the trade is at least +$0.01 after commission; or Jim's ladder (peak +$0.03 locks +$0.01, +$0.09 -> $0.045, +$0.15 -> $0.10, +$0.30 -> $0.15, +$0.50 -> $0.25, +$1.00 -> $0.80, above $1 keeps 80%; checked every ~3 s, closes at market); or 15 minutes; or the broker hard stop at 2x the entry gap (at least 2x spread + 1 pip, max $1). Max 3 open, 1 per cross. Auto-stop at -$150 or 5 losses in a row. All hours FX is open; Friday 4:50 PM ET flatten (no entries after 4:35). FTMO guards: equity below day-start - $8,000 or below $182,000 -> flatten, no entries. · runner running · placing ON · last check Fri 10/9 9:15 AM ET
No trades yet, so every $0.00 / 0% below is a real zero, not missing data. Verdict: Still slightly negative after the fixes. Recommend: do not re-arm at 0.05+. If Jim wants it running, use 0.01 only.

What was wrong

  • Since the 8:56 PM ET re-arm: 5 closes at 0.05, -$3.18. The 3 green closes were only +$0.04, +$0.24 and +$0.04. The trailing lock fires about 0.4 pip after the peak, and the web close lag eats most of that (for example, trigger +$0.23 closed at +$0.05).
  • The 2 red closes were both 30-minute no-progress exits: USDJPY -$2.03 and EURGBP -$1.47. That exit closes at a loss before the trade has room. It was the worst exit in the backtest (as live: OOS PF 0.56, against 0.66 without it).
  • The lot ladder floor was 0.05, so a red close could never step the lot down. It started at 5x on an entry that is negative in the backtest.
  • It traded at all hours. The all-hours backtest is much worse than 2-4 PM ET.

What was changed

  • 30-min no-progress exit removed.
  • New entries 2:00-4:00 PM ET only. No entries 4:45-6:15 PM ET or on Friday after 3:30 PM ET.
  • Lot ladder is 0.01-0.05-0.10-0.20-0.35-0.50. It starts at 0.01 and steps down to a 0.01 floor. It only steps up after the promotion gate is met (30+ trades, MTM consistency 75% or more, median above $0, best trade under 50% of profit).
  • Runner stopped and switch off since 5:42 AM ET 10/9. No trades were open.

0.01 lot · tag BASKETV2 · Three methods in one book, each order tagged with its own comment so results split per method. V (BASKETV2V) = the Ping 1 V-turn on M3. R (BASKETV2R) = RSI(2) on M15 below 5 while H1 is above its 50-EMA (a sharp dip against an up-trend: buy it), or above 95 while H1 is below its 50-EMA (a spike against a down-trend: sell it). X (BASKETV2X) = Mistake-style: a cross's price drifts away from the price implied by its two USD legs by more than costs; trade it back, exit on convergence (net >= +$0.01) or after 15 min, stop 2x the gap (max $1). 5 · Details

Three methods in one book, each order tagged with its own comment so results split per method. V (BASKETV2V) = the Ping 1 V-turn on M3. R (BASKETV2R) = RSI(2) on M15 below 5 while H1 is above its 50-EMA (a sharp dip against an up-trend: buy it), or above 95 while H1 is below its 50-EMA (a spike against a down-trend: sell it). X (BASKETV2X) = Mistake-style: a cross's price drifts away from the price implied by its two USD legs by more than costs; trade it back, exit on convergence (net >= +$0.01) or after 15 min, stop 2x the gap (max $1). At most one trade per method per pair, max 8 open, 0.01 lot.

5 · Details

PING1-style V-turn entry. Every trade gets a defined stop of 6 x ATR(M3) (at least 2x spread + 1 pip). The lot is sized so that stop costs at most $0.50: if 0.01 at that stop would cost more, the stop is tightened to the $0.50 distance (skipped if that would be tighter than 2x spread + 1 pip); if the stop is small, the lot grows (rounded down) up to 0.03. Target 6x the stop, Jim's trailing ladder. Hard daily stop for this test: -$3 (closed + open since arming) -> flatten and pause. fixed Fri 5:49:17 AM ET

What was wrong

  • Lot-sizing bug: the lot was computed 100x too big (1.00 lot, clipped to 0.03), so every trade looked like it risked 3x the $0.50 budget and was skipped. That is why it had 0 trades on 10/8 (GBPUSD and USDJPY at 4:36 PM ET both showed $1.50 risk).

  • Budget left at $0.50. At $0.50 a 0.01 lot only fits by tightening the stop to about 5 pips on EURUSD (about 2x ATR M3). The intended 6x ATR stop costs $1.20-$2.10 at 0.01, so it would need about $1.50.
